been a little while since our last update, but we've been hard at work growing Sienna Sauce. As a company with limited capital in the capital-intensive retail industry, we've made a strategic decision to shift focus towards growing our e-commerce presence. We're thrilled to share that Sienna Sauce is now available on Amazon Prime and TikTok Shop, along with the launch of our brand-new website and a newly acquired shipping facility. These moves allow us to reach more customers directly while still maintaining strong relationships with our existing retailers.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe remain heavily involved with H-E-B and Hy-Vee and are looking forward to expanding both our product line and store presence within these chains. Additionally, we're actively pursuing other strategic partnerships with regional retailers, focusing on first dominating the Texas market before expanding nationally as we acquire more capital.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe journey so far has been full of learning experiences, and we’re incredibly grateful to be one of the few companies at our stage to have made it through the challenges of COVID-19. We deeply appreciate the support and investment from all of you, and if you ever come across any potential opportunities, please don't hesitate to reach out. We are especially interested in exploring partnerships in the food service and commercial dining sectors.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn other exciting news, our award-winning Lemon Pepper Sauce will be featured at the MGM Mandalay Bay in Las Vegas from November 16th to December 13th, thanks to an exciting partnership our founder has secured with Pepsi. This residency is a great opportunity to introduce our sauce to new audiences, and we’re optimistic about what might follow from this exposure.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs we move into the fourth quarter, we are focused on maximizing the upcoming event season and positioning Sienna Sauce for continued success. raise over $200,000! It took a lot of hard work, patience, but most importantly, it took you believing in us - in our mission, and today the real work begins. It's time for us to go harder, grow more prominent, and show you our investors that your reward will be worth the risk.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eHere's what you can look forward to as our new investor:\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cp\u003eInvestor Perk:\u0026nbsp;As stated on our campaign with each level of investment, you receive several \"thank you\" perks. Once the campaign's logistics are settled, you can expect your gifts in the mail within the next 30 - 60 days. FYI, your initial investment perk of 20% off our sauce, will expire on August 1st CST.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cp\u003eSienna Sauce Subscription Program: Customers will now be able to purchase Sienna Sauce on subscription, launching this month. Our sauce lovers can choose between 3 flavors of the sauce, sent to them regularly at the frequency of their choosing. The subscription program will include our new e-cookbook (to the first 100 subscribers), free shipping, and packaged in our new boxes!\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cp\u003eNew Product Launch: This one is a secret, but since you're now apart of the Sienna Sauce family, we're letting you in on it. This quarter we'll be launching a new product! We can't share too many details, but we're in the final production stages, and super excited for you all to see it. You can look forward to more exciting news about our new product!\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eNow being an investor, you must be wondering what we plan to do with your contribution. First on the agenda is growing our team. Our sauce may have\u0026nbsp;big\u0026nbsp;flavor, but we're still a small business that's proliferating. As we grow, your capital will go towards hiring staff to keep Sienna Sauce running efficiently and competing in the ever-expanding condiment industry. Speaking of growing - remember that new product launch we mentioned above, well, that's just the first of many! We're going to take the condiment industry by storm with our unique flavors, and with you backing us, Sienna Sauce will do just that. To ensure our success, marketing and advertising of the Sienna Sauce brand is a must. As we've already started expanding our marketing efforts, your investment will further develop Sienna Sauce's marketing team and strategy.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLastly, we want to say Thank you!
